     1→#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2→"""
     3→Graph Context — Token-efficient vault context via SurrealDB graph traversal.
     4→
     5→Queries the vault's neuron/synapse graph and returns compact, agent-ready context.
     6→Designed for minimal token consumption: one call replaces reading multiple files.
     7→
     8→Usage:
     9→    graph_context.py neighborhood <query>     # Local neighborhood of a neuron
    10→    graph_context.py search <query>           # Find neurons by title/tag match
    11→    graph_context.py cluster <name>           # Cluster summary with top neurons
    12→    graph_context.py hops <query> [depth]     # N-hop traversal (default: 2)
    13→    graph_context.py bridges <cluster_a> <cluster_b>  # Cross-domain connectors
    14→    graph_context.py stats                    # Global vault health snapshot
    15→    graph_context.py resolve <query>          # Find neuron ID from partial name
    16→    graph_context.py briefing                  # Full vault briefing for agent context
    17→
    18→Output is plain text, optimized for agent context injection.
    19→"""
    20→
    21→import base64
    22→import json
    23→import sys
    24→import urllib.request
    25→
    26→SURREAL_URL = "http://localhost:8001/sql"
    27→SURREAL_NS = "cohezion"
    28→SURREAL_DB = "vault"
    29→SURREAL_USER = "root"
    30→SURREAL_PASS = "root"
    31→
    32→
    33→def query(sql: str) -> list:
    34→    """Execute SurrealQL and return result list."""
    35→    creds = base64.b64encode(f"{SURREAL_USER}:{SURREAL_PASS}".encode()).decode()
    36→    req = urllib.request.Request(
    37→        SURREAL_URL,
    38→        data=sql.encode("utf-8"),
    39→        headers={
    40→            "Accept": "application/json",
    41→            "Content-Type": "text/plain",
    42→            "Authorization": f"Basic {creds}",
    43→            "surreal-ns": SURREAL_NS,
    44→            "surreal-db": SURREAL_DB,
    45→        },
    46→        method="POST",
    47→    )
    48→    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=10) as resp:
    49→        data = json.loads(resp.read())
    50→    if isinstance(data, dict) and "code" in data:
    51→        print(f"Error: {data.get('description', data)}", file=sys.stderr)
    52→        sys.exit(1)
    53→    return data
    54→
    55→
    56→def resolve_neuron(partial: str) -> str | None:
    57→    """Find a neuron ID from a partial name match."""
    58→    sql = f"SELECT id, title, activation FROM neuron WHERE string::contains(string::lowercase(title), string::lowercase('{_esc(partial)}')) ORDER BY activation DESC LIMIT 5;"
    59→    results = query(sql)
    60→    hits = results[0].get("result", [])
    61→    if not hits:
    62→        return None
    63→    return str(hits[0]["id"])
    64→
    65→
    66→def _esc(s: str) -> str:
    67→    """Escape single quotes and backslashes for SurrealQL strings."""
    68→    return s.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace("'", "\\'")
    69→
    70→
    71→def _act_bar(activation: float) -> str:
    72→    """Compact activation bar: 0.85 → [████░]"""
    73→    filled = round(activation * 5)
    74→    return "[" + "\u2588" * filled + "\u2591" * (5 - filled) + "]"
    75→
    76→
    77→def _format_neuron_line(n: dict, prefix: str = "") -> str:
    78→    """Format a single neuron as a compact line."""
    79→    act = n.get("activation", 0)
    80→    title = n.get("title", "?")
    81→    stage = n.get("stage", "?")
    82→    cluster = n.get("cluster_id", n.get("cluster", ""))
    83→    syn_out = n.get("synapse_out", 0)
    84→    syn_in = n.get("synapse_in", 0)
    85→    return f"{prefix}{_act_bar(act)} {act:.2f} {title} ({stage}, {cluster}, out:{syn_out} in:{syn_in})"
    86→
    87→
    88→def cmd_neighborhood(args: list[str]):
    89→    partial = " ".join(args)
    90→    nid = resolve_neuron(partial)
    91→    if not nid:
    92→        print(f"No neuron found matching '{partial}'")
    93→        sys.exit(1)
    94→
    95→    sql = f"SELECT * FROM fn::context_neighborhood({nid});"
    96→    results = query(sql)
    97→    data = results[0]["result"][0]
    98→    n = data["neuron"]
    99→
   100→    # Workaround: SurrealDB functions don't preserve ORDER BY,
   101→    # so fetch cluster_top via direct query
   102→    cluster_sql = f"SELECT id, title, activation, stage FROM neuron WHERE cluster_id = '{_esc(n['cluster_id'])}' AND id != {nid} ORDER BY activation DESC LIMIT 5;"
   103→    cluster_results = query(cluster_sql)
   104→    data["cluster_top"] = cluster_results[0]["result"]
   105→
   106→    print(f"=== {n['title']} ===")
   107→    print(f"Path: {n['path']}")
   108→    print(f"Activation: {n['activation']:.3f} | Stage: {n['stage']} | Aspect: {n['aspect']}")
   109→    print(f"Cluster: {n['cluster_id']} | Words: {n['word_count']}")
   110→    print(f"Tags: {', '.join(n.get('tags', []))}")
   111→    print(f"Synapses: out={n['synapse_out']} in={n['synapse_in']}")
   112→
   113→    if data["outbound"]:
   114→        print(f"\n--- Outbound ({len(data['outbound'])}) ---")
   115→        for link in sorted(data["outbound"], key=lambda x: x.get("activation", 0), reverse=True):
   116→            print(_format_neuron_line(link, "  -> "))
   117→
   118→    if data["inbound"]:
   119→        print(f"\n--- Inbound ({len(data['inbound'])}) ---")
   120→        for link in sorted(data["inbound"], key=lambda x: x.get("activation", 0), reverse=True):
   121→            print(_format_neuron_line(link, "  <- "))
   122→
   123→    if data["cluster_top"]:
   124→        print(f"\n--- Cluster Siblings (top 5 in '{n['cluster_id']}') ---")
   125→        for sib in sorted(data["cluster_top"], key=lambda x: x.get("activation", 0), reverse=True):
   126→            print(f"  {_act_bar(sib['activation'])} {sib['activation']:.2f} {sib['title']}")
   127→
   128→
   129→def cmd_search(args: list[str]):
   130→    q = " ".join(args)
   131→    sql = f"SELECT * FROM fn::context_search('{_esc(q)}');"
   132→    results = query(sql)
   133→    hits = results[0]["result"]
   134→    if not hits:
   135→        print(f"No neurons matching '{q}'")
   136→        return
   137→    print(f"=== Search: '{q}' ({len(hits)} results) ===")
   138→    for n in hits:
   139→        print(_format_neuron_line(n))
   140→
   141→
   142→def cmd_cluster(args: list[str]):
   143→    name = args[0] if args else "cortex"
   144→    sql = f"SELECT * FROM fn::context_cluster('{_esc(name)}');"
   145→    results = query(sql)
   146→    data = results[0]["result"][0]
   147→
   148→    # Workaround: SurrealDB functions don't preserve ORDER BY,
   149→    # so fetch top_neurons via direct query
   150→    top_sql = f"SELECT id, title, activation, stage, synapse_out, synapse_in FROM neuron WHERE cluster_id = '{_esc(name)}' ORDER BY activation DESC LIMIT 10;"
   151→    top_results = query(top_sql)
   152→    data["top_neurons"] = top_results[0]["result"]
   153→
   154→    print(f"=== Cluster: {data['cluster']} ===")
   155→    print(f"Neurons: {data['total_neurons']} | Avg Activation: {data['avg_activation']:.3f} | Coherence: {data['coherence']:.3f}")
   156→    print(f"\n--- Top Neurons ---")
   157→    for n in data["top_neurons"]:
   158→        print(_format_neuron_line(n, "  "))
   159→
   160→
   161→def cmd_hops(args: list[str]):
   162→    partial = args[0] if args else ""
   163→    depth = int(args[1]) if len(args) > 1 else 2
   164→    nid = resolve_neuron(partial)
   165→    if not nid:
   166→        print(f"No neuron found matching '{partial}'")
   167→        sys.exit(1)
   168→
   169→    sql = f"SELECT * FROM fn::context_hops({nid}, {depth});"
   170→    results = query(sql)
   171→    hits = results[0]["result"]
   172→    print(f"=== {depth}-hop neighborhood ({len(hits)} neurons) ===")
   173→    for n in hits:
   174→        print(_format_neuron_line(n, "  "))
   175→
   176→
   177→def cmd_bridges(args: list[str]):
   178→    if len(args) < 2:
   179→        print("Usage: bridges <cluster_a> <cluster_b>")
   180→        sys.exit(1)
   181→    sql = f"SELECT * FROM fn::context_bridges('{_esc(args[0])}', '{_esc(args[1])}');"
   182→    results = query(sql)
   183→    hits = results[0]["result"]
   184→    print(f"=== Bridges: {args[0]} <-> {args[1]} ({len(hits)} connectors) ===")
   185→    for n in hits:
   186→        print(_format_neuron_line(n, "  "))
   187→
   188→
   189→def cmd_stats(args: list[str]):
   190→    sql = "SELECT * FROM fn::vault_stats();"
   191→    results = query(sql)
   192→    data = results[0]["result"][0]
   193→
   194→    def _unwrap_count(v):
   195→        if isinstance(v, list) and v and isinstance(v[0], dict):
   196→            return v[0].get("count", v)
   197→        return v
   198→
   199→    print(f"=== Vault Stats ===")
   200→    print(f"Neurons: {_unwrap_count(data['total_neurons'])} | Synapses: {_unwrap_count(data['total_synapses'])}")
   201→
   202→    print(f"\n--- Stage Distribution ---")
   203→    for s in data["stage_distribution"]:
   204→        print(f"  {s['stage']}: {s['n']}")
   205→
   206→    print(f"\n--- Clusters (top 10) ---")
   207→    for c in data["clusters"][:10]:
   208→        print(f"  {c['cluster_id']}: {c['n']} neurons (avg act: {c['avg_act']:.3f})")
   209→
   210→
   211→def cmd_resolve(args: list[str]):
   212→    partial = " ".join(args)
   213→    sql = f"SELECT id, title, activation, path FROM neuron WHERE string::contains(string::lowercase(title), string::lowercase('{_esc(partial)}')) ORDER BY activation DESC LIMIT 10;"
   214→    results = query(sql)
   215→    hits = results[0].get("result", [])
   216→    if not hits:
   217→        print(f"No neurons matching '{partial}'")
   218→        return
   219→    for n in hits:
   220→        print(f"  {n['activation']:.2f} {n['id']} — {n['title']}")
   221→
   222→
   223→def cmd_briefing(args: list[str]):
   224→    """Generate a compact graph briefing for agent context injection."""
   225→    from datetime import datetime, timezone
   226→
   227→    ts = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
   228→    print(f"# Graph Briefing — {ts}\n")
   229→
   230→    # Vitals
   231→    stats_sql = "SELECT count() AS n FROM neuron GROUP ALL; SELECT count() AS n FROM synapse GROUP ALL;"
   232→    stats = query(stats_sql)
   233→    n_neurons = stats[0]["result"][0]["n"] if stats[0]["result"] else 0
   234→    n_synapses = stats[1]["result"][0]["n"] if stats[1]["result"] else 0
   235→
   236→    stage_sql = "SELECT stage, count() AS n FROM neuron GROUP BY stage;"
   237→    stages = query(stage_sql)[0]["result"]
   238→    stage_str = ", ".join(f"{s['n']} {s['stage']}" for s in stages)
   239→
   240→    print("## Vitals")
   241→    print(f"Neurons: {n_neurons} | Synapses: {n_synapses}")
   242→    print(f"Stages: {stage_str}\n")
   243→
   244→    # Hot neurons (top 15)
   245→    hot_sql = "SELECT id, title, activation, stage, cluster_id, synapse_out, synapse_in FROM neuron ORDER BY activation DESC LIMIT 15;"
   246→    hot = query(hot_sql)[0]["result"]
   247→    print("## Hot Neurons (top 15)")
   248→    for n in hot:
   249→        print(_format_neuron_line(n))
   250→    print()
   251→
   252→    # Cross-domain bridges (high connectivity neurons)
   253→    bridge_sql = (
   254→        "SELECT id, title, activation, stage, cluster_id, synapse_out, synapse_in "
   255→        "FROM neuron WHERE synapse_out > 3 AND synapse_in > 1 "
   256→        "ORDER BY synapse_out DESC LIMIT 10;"
   257→    )
   258→    bridges = query(bridge_sql)[0]["result"]
   259→    if bridges:
   260→        print("## Cross-Domain Bridges (top 10 by connectivity)")
   261→        for n in bridges:
   262→            print(_format_neuron_line(n))
   263→        print()
   264→
   265→    # Attention needed
   266→    embryo_sql = "SELECT id, title, activation FROM neuron WHERE stage = 'embryo' ORDER BY activation DESC LIMIT 5;"
   267→    embryos = query(embryo_sql)[0]["result"]
   268→    orphan_sql = "SELECT id, title FROM neuron WHERE synapse_out = 0 AND synapse_in = 0 LIMIT 5;"
   269→    orphans = query(orphan_sql)[0]["result"]
   270→
   271→    print("## Attention Needed")
   272→    n_embryos = next((s["n"] for s in stages if s["stage"] == "embryo"), 0)
   273→    print(f"- {n_embryos} embryo notes" + (f" (top: {', '.join(e['title'] for e in embryos[:3])})" if embryos else ""))
   274→    if orphans:
   275→        print(f"- {len(orphans)} disconnected neurons: {', '.join(o['title'] for o in orphans[:3])}")
   276→    print()
   277→
   278→    # Highest energy
   279→    energy_sql = (
   280→        "SELECT id, title, activation, stage, cluster_id, synapse_out, synapse_in "
   281→        "FROM neuron WHERE activation >= 0.9 ORDER BY activation DESC LIMIT 10;"
   282→    )
   283→    energy = query(energy_sql)[0]["result"]
   284→    if energy:
   285→        print("## Highest Energy")
   286→        for n in energy:
   287→            print(_format_neuron_line(n))
   288→        print()
   289→
   290→
   291→COMMANDS = {
   292→    "neighborhood": cmd_neighborhood,
   293→    "n": cmd_neighborhood,
   294→    "search": cmd_search,
   295→    "s": cmd_search,
   296→    "cluster": cmd_cluster,
   297→    "c": cmd_cluster,
   298→    "hops": cmd_hops,
   299→    "h": cmd_hops,
   300→    "bridges": cmd_bridges,
   301→    "b": cmd_bridges,
   302→    "stats": cmd_stats,
   303→    "resolve": cmd_resolve,
   304→    "r": cmd_resolve,
   305→    "briefing": cmd_briefing,
   306→}
   307→
   308→
   309→def main():
   310→    if len(sys.argv) < 2 or sys.argv[1] in ("--help", "-h"):
   311→        print(__doc__.strip())
   312→        sys.exit(0)
   313→
   314→    cmd = sys.argv[1]
   315→    if cmd not in COMMANDS:
   316→        print(f"Unknown command: {cmd}")
   317→        print(f"Available: {', '.join(k for k in COMMANDS if len(k) > 1)}")
   318→        sys.exit(1)
   319→
   320→    COMMANDS[cmd](sys.argv[2:])
   321→
   322→
   323→if __name__ == "__main__":
   324→    main()
   325→

     1→-- Context Query Functions for Cohezion Vault Graph
     2→-- These functions return token-efficient context for agent consumption.
     3→-- Install: surreal import --conn http://localhost:8001 --ns cohezion --db vault context_functions.surql
     4→
     5→-- ─── fn::context_neighborhood ────────────────────────────────────────────────
     6→-- Returns the immediate neighborhood of a neuron: its metadata, outbound
     7→-- targets, inbound sources, and cluster siblings. One call, full local context.
     8→--
     9→-- Usage: SELECT * FROM fn::context_neighborhood(neuron:cortex_sarfatti_post_quantum_mechanics_and_toe_md);
    10→DEFINE FUNCTION fn::context_neighborhood($nid: record<neuron>) {
    11→    LET $n = (SELECT title, activation, stage, cluster_id, aspect, tags,
    12→                     synapse_out, synapse_in, word_count, path
    13→              FROM ONLY $nid);
    14→
    15→    LET $outbound = (SELECT out.id AS id, out.title AS title,
    16→                            out.activation AS activation, out.stage AS stage,
    17→                            out.cluster_id AS cluster
    18→                     FROM synapse WHERE in = $nid);
    19→
    20→    LET $inbound  = (SELECT in.id AS id, in.title AS title,
    21→                            in.activation AS activation, in.stage AS stage,
    22→                            in.cluster_id AS cluster
    23→                     FROM synapse WHERE out = $nid);
    24→
    25→    LET $cluster_top = (SELECT id, title, activation, stage
    26→                        FROM neuron
    27→                        WHERE cluster_id = $n.cluster_id AND id != $nid
    28→                        ORDER BY activation DESC LIMIT 5);
    29→
    30→    RETURN {
    31→        neuron: $n,
    32→        outbound: $outbound,
    33→        inbound: $inbound,
    34→        cluster_top: $cluster_top
    35→    };
    36→};
    37→
    38→-- ─── fn::context_cluster ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    39→-- Returns a cluster summary: top neurons by activation, total count, average
    40→-- activation, coherence score. Useful for "what's in this domain?"
    41→--
    42→-- Usage: SELECT * FROM fn::context_cluster("cortex");
    43→DEFINE FUNCTION fn::context_cluster($cluster_name: string) {
    44→    LET $neurons = (SELECT id, title, activation, stage, synapse_out, synapse_in
    45→                    FROM neuron
    46→                    WHERE cluster_id = $cluster_name
    47→                    ORDER BY activation DESC);
    48→
    49→    LET $total = array::len($neurons);
    50→    LET $avg_act = math::mean($neurons.activation);
    51→    LET $coherence = fn::compute_coherence($cluster_name);
    52→
    53→    LET $top = (SELECT id, title, activation, stage, synapse_out, synapse_in
    54→                FROM neuron
    55→                WHERE cluster_id = $cluster_name
    56→                ORDER BY activation DESC LIMIT 10);
    57→
    58→    RETURN {
    59→        cluster: $cluster_name,
    60→        total_neurons: $total,
    61→        avg_activation: $avg_act,
    62→        coherence: $coherence,
    63→        top_neurons: $top
    64→    };
    65→};
    66→
    67→-- ─── fn::context_search ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    68→-- Finds neurons by title substring match, returns compact results sorted by
    69→-- activation. Use for "find everything related to X" queries.
    70→--
    71→-- Usage: SELECT * FROM fn::context_search("quantum");
    72→DEFINE FUNCTION fn::context_search($query: string) {
    73→    LET $matches = (SELECT id, title, activation, stage, cluster_id, aspect,
    74→                           synapse_out, synapse_in, path
    75→                    FROM neuron
    76→                    WHERE string::contains(string::lowercase(title), string::lowercase($query))
    77→                       OR string::contains(string::lowercase(<string> tags), string::lowercase($query))
    78→                    ORDER BY activation DESC LIMIT 20);
    79→
    80→    RETURN $matches;
    81→};
    82→
    83→-- ─── fn::context_hops ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    84→-- Returns all neurons reachable within N hops from a starting neuron.
    85→-- Hop 1 = direct neighbors, Hop 2 = neighbors of neighbors.
    86→-- Returns deduplicated results sorted by activation.
    87→--
    88→-- Usage: SELECT * FROM fn::context_hops(neuron:cortex_sarfatti_post_quantum_mechanics_and_toe_md, 2);
    89→DEFINE FUNCTION fn::context_hops($nid: record<neuron>, $depth: int) {
    90→    -- Hop 1: direct connections (both directions)
    91→    LET $hop1_ids = array::union(
    92→        (SELECT VALUE out FROM synapse WHERE in = $nid),
    93→        (SELECT VALUE in FROM synapse WHERE out = $nid)
    94→    );
    95→
    96→    IF $depth <= 1 {
    97→        RETURN (SELECT id, title, activation, stage, cluster_id, synapse_out, synapse_in
    98→                FROM neuron WHERE id IN $hop1_ids
    99→                ORDER BY activation DESC);
   100→    };
   101→
   102→    -- Hop 2: connections of hop1 neurons
   103→    LET $hop2_ids = array::union(
   104→        (SELECT VALUE out FROM synapse WHERE in IN $hop1_ids AND out != $nid AND out NOT IN $hop1_ids),
   105→        (SELECT VALUE in FROM synapse WHERE out IN $hop1_ids AND in != $nid AND in NOT IN $hop1_ids)
   106→    );
   107→
   108→    LET $all_ids = array::union($hop1_ids, $hop2_ids);
   109→
   110→    RETURN (SELECT id, title, activation, stage, cluster_id, synapse_out, synapse_in
   111→            FROM neuron WHERE id IN $all_ids
   112→            ORDER BY activation DESC LIMIT 50);
   113→};
   114→
   115→-- ─── fn::context_bridges ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   116→-- Finds neurons that bridge two clusters — nodes with synapses into both.
   117→-- These are the cross-domain connectors, the most token-valuable context.
   118→--
   119→-- Usage: SELECT * FROM fn::context_bridges("cortex", "sensory");
   120→DEFINE FUNCTION fn::context_bridges($cluster_a: string, $cluster_b: string) {
   121→    -- Neurons in cluster_a that link to cluster_b
   122→    LET $a_to_b = (SELECT VALUE in FROM synapse
   123→                   WHERE in.cluster_id = $cluster_a AND out.cluster_id = $cluster_b);
   124→
   125→    -- Neurons in cluster_b that link to cluster_a
   126→    LET $b_to_a = (SELECT VALUE in FROM synapse
   127→                   WHERE in.cluster_id = $cluster_b AND out.cluster_id = $cluster_a);
   128→
   129→    LET $bridge_ids = array::union($a_to_b, $b_to_a);
   130→
   131→    RETURN (SELECT id, title, activation, stage, cluster_id, synapse_out, synapse_in, path
   132→            FROM neuron WHERE id IN $bridge_ids
   133→            ORDER BY activation DESC LIMIT 20);
   134→};
   135→
   136→-- ─── fn::vault_stats ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   137→-- Global vault health snapshot. One call for dashboard-level awareness.
   138→--
   139→-- Usage: SELECT * FROM fn::vault_stats();
   140→DEFINE FUNCTION fn::vault_stats() {
   141→    LET $total = array::len((SELECT id FROM neuron));
   142→    LET $synapses = array::len((SELECT id FROM synapse));
   143→    LET $clusters = (SELECT cluster_id, count() AS n, math::mean(activation) AS avg_act
   144→                     FROM neuron GROUP BY cluster_id ORDER BY n DESC);
   145→    LET $stages = (SELECT stage, count() AS n FROM neuron GROUP BY stage);
   146→
   147→    RETURN {
   148→        total_neurons: $total[0].n,
   149→        total_synapses: $synapses[0].n,
   150→        clusters: $clusters,
   151→        stage_distribution: $stages
   152→    };
   153→};
   154→

     1→#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2→"""
     3→Graph Context Hook — inject graph awareness into agent prompts.
     4→
     5→Runs as a UserPromptSubmit hook. Extracts keywords from the user's prompt,
     6→queries the SurrealDB graph, and prints compact context to stdout.
     7→Silent (zero output) when no neurons match or SurrealDB is unavailable.
     8→
     9→Environment: $CLAUDE_USER_PROMPT contains the user's prompt text.
    10→"""
    11→
    12→import base64
    13→import json
    14→import os
    15→import re
    16→import sys
    17→import urllib.request
    18→
    19→SURREAL_URL = "http://localhost:8001/sql"
    20→SURREAL_NS = "cohezion"
    21→SURREAL_DB = "vault"
    22→SURREAL_USER = "root"
    23→SURREAL_PASS = "root"
    24→
    25→SKIP_PATTERNS = re.compile(
    26→    r"^(yes|no|y|n|continue|proceed|commit|push|commit and push|"
    27→    r"/\w+.*|ok|done|stop|cancel|help|thanks|thank you|"
    28→    r"compound it|do it|go|go ahead|lgtm|ship it|d|c|"
    29→    r"\d+)$",
    30→    re.IGNORECASE,
    31→)
    32→
    33→STOPWORDS = frozenset(
    34→    "a an the is are was were be been being have has had do does did "
    35→    "will would shall should may might can could of in to for on with "
    36→    "at by from as into through during before after above below between "
    37→    "and or but not no nor so yet both either neither each every all "
    38→    "any few more most other some such than too very just about also "
    39→    "back how its let me my our out own same she that their them then "
    40→    "there these they this those up us we what when where which while "
    41→    "who whom why you your i he him his her it we make run add fix "
    42→    "use get set put new old can tell show look find read write edit "
    43→    "create update delete check test pick path".split()
    44→)
    45→
    46→
    47→def surreal_query(sql: str) -> list | None:
    48→    """Execute SurrealQL, return results or None on failure."""
    49→    try:
    50→        creds = base64.b64encode(f"{SURREAL_USER}:{SURREAL_PASS}".encode()).decode()
    51→        req = urllib.request.Request(
    52→            SURREAL_URL,
    53→            data=sql.encode("utf-8"),
    54→            headers={
    55→                "Accept": "application/json",
    56→                "Content-Type": "text/plain",
    57→                "Authorization": f"Basic {creds}",
    58→                "surreal-ns": SURREAL_NS,
    59→                "surreal-db": SURREAL_DB,
    60→            },
    61→            method="POST",
    62→        )
    63→        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=3) as resp:
    64→            data = json.loads(resp.read())
    65→        if isinstance(data, dict) and "code" in data:
    66→            return None
    67→        return data
    68→    except Exception:
    69→        return None
    70→
    71→
    72→def extract_keywords(prompt: str) -> list[str]:
    73→    """Extract candidate keywords from a user prompt."""
    74→    clean = re.sub(r"[^\w\s-]", " ", prompt.lower())
    75→    words = [w for w in clean.split() if w not in STOPWORDS and len(w) > 2]
    76→
    77→    if not words:
    78→        return []
    79→
    80→    candidates = []
    81→
    82→    # 3-word windows (best for multi-word note titles)
    83→    for i in range(len(words) - 2):
    84→        candidates.append(" ".join(words[i : i + 3]))
    85→
    86→    # 2-word windows
    87→    for i in range(len(words) - 1):
    88→        candidates.append(" ".join(words[i : i + 2]))
    89→
    90→    # Single substantial words
    91→    for w in words:
    92→        if len(w) > 3:
    93→            candidates.append(w)
    94→
    95→    return candidates[:12]
    96→
    97→
    98→def _esc(s: str) -> str:
    99→    return s.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace("'", "\\'")
   100→
   101→
   102→def find_best_match(candidates: list[str]) -> dict | None:
   103→    """Try candidates against the graph, best match by candidate priority then activation."""
   104→    # Batch all candidates into a single query — one round-trip instead of N
   105→    conditions = " OR ".join(
   106→        f"string::contains(string::lowercase(title), '{_esc(c)}')"
   107→        for c in candidates
   108→    )
   109→    sql = (
   110→        f"SELECT id, title, activation, stage, cluster_id, aspect, "
   111→        f"tags, synapse_out, synapse_in, path "
   112→        f"FROM neuron WHERE {conditions} "
   113→        f"ORDER BY activation DESC LIMIT 20;"
   114→    )
   115→    results = surreal_query(sql)
   116→    if not results or not results[0].get("result"):
   117→        return None
   118→
   119→    hits = results[0]["result"]
   120→    # Return hit matching the earliest (highest-priority) candidate
   121→    for candidate in candidates:
   122→        for hit in hits:
   123→            if candidate in hit.get("title", "").lower():
   124→                return hit
   125→    # Fallback: highest activation among all hits
   126→    return hits[0]
   127→
   128→
   129→def get_connections(neuron_id: str) -> tuple[list, list]:
   130→    """Get outbound and inbound connections for a neuron."""
   131→    sql = (
   132→        f"SELECT out.title AS title, out.activation AS activation, "
   133→        f"out.stage AS stage, out.cluster_id AS cluster, out.path AS path "
   134→        f"FROM synapse WHERE in = {neuron_id} "
   135→        f"ORDER BY out.activation DESC LIMIT 5;\n"
   136→        f"SELECT in.title AS title, in.activation AS activation, "
   137→        f"in.stage AS stage, in.cluster_id AS cluster, in.path AS path "
   138→        f"FROM synapse WHERE out = {neuron_id} "
   139→        f"ORDER BY in.activation DESC LIMIT 5;"
   140→    )
   141→    results = surreal_query(sql)
   142→    if not results or len(results) < 2:
   143→        return [], []
   144→    return results[0].get("result", []), results[1].get("result", [])
   145→
   146→
   147→def _act_bar(activation: float) -> str:
   148→    filled = round(activation * 5)
   149→    return "[" + "\u2588" * filled + "\u2591" * (5 - filled) + "]"
   150→
   151→
   152→def format_output(neuron: dict, outbound: list, inbound: list) -> str:
   153→    """Format compact context output."""
   154→    act = neuron.get("activation", 0)
   155→    title = neuron.get("title", "?")
   156→    stage = neuron.get("stage", "?")
   157→    cluster = neuron.get("cluster_id", "?")
   158→    s_out = neuron.get("synapse_out", 0)
   159→    s_in = neuron.get("synapse_in", 0)
   160→    path = neuron.get("path", "?")
   161→    tags = neuron.get("tags", [])
   162→
   163→    lines = [f'--- Graph Context: "{title}" ---']
   164→    lines.append(f"{_act_bar(act)} {act:.2f} {title} ({stage}, {cluster}, out:{s_out} in:{s_in})")
   165→    lines.append(f"  Path: {path}")
   166→    if tags:
   167→        lines.append(f"  Tags: {', '.join(str(t) for t in tags[:6])}")
   168→
   169→    for link in outbound[:5]:
   170→        la = link.get("activation", 0)
   171→        lines.append(f"  -> {_act_bar(la)} {la:.2f} {link.get('title', '?')} ({link.get('cluster', '?')})")
   172→
   173→    for link in inbound[:5]:
   174→        la = link.get("activation", 0)
   175→        lines.append(f"  <- {_act_bar(la)} {la:.2f} {link.get('title', '?')} ({link.get('cluster', '?')})")
   176→
   177→    # Read list: matched neuron path + top 2 connected paths by activation
   178→    read_paths = []
   179→    if path and path != "?":
   180→        read_paths.append(path)
   181→    connected = sorted(
   182→        [l for l in (outbound + inbound) if l.get("path")],
   183→        key=lambda l: l.get("activation", 0),
   184→        reverse=True,
   185→    )
   186→    for link in connected:
   187→        p = link.get("path", "")
   188→        if p and p not in read_paths:
   189→            read_paths.append(p)
   190→        if len(read_paths) >= 3:
   191→            break
   192→    if read_paths:
   193→        lines.append(f"  Read: {' | '.join(read_paths)}")
   194→
   195→    lines.append("\u2500" * 40)
   196→    return "\n".join(lines)
   197→
   198→
   199→def main():
   200→    prompt = os.environ.get("CLAUDE_USER_PROMPT", "").strip()
   201→    if not prompt:
   202→        return
   203→
   204→    if SKIP_PATTERNS.match(prompt):
   205→        return
   206→
   207→    if len(prompt) < 8:
   208→        return
   209→
   210→    keywords = extract_keywords(prompt)
   211→    if not keywords:
   212→        return
   213→
   214→    neuron = find_best_match(keywords)
   215→    if not neuron:
   216→        return
   217→
   218→    neuron_id = str(neuron["id"])
   219→    outbound, inbound = get_connections(neuron_id)
   220→    print(format_output(neuron, outbound, inbound))
   221→
   222→
   223→if __name__ == "__main__":
   224→    main()
   225→

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
The hook (`graph_context_hook.py`) and the CLI (`graph_context.py`) are two separate scripts that both duplicate the same urllib pattern — both hardcode `root:root` credentials that differ from the systemd service's `sdb_admin_session43` user. The SDK migration in Phase A fixes this by unifying the connection config through env vars.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

  "content": "# Graph Architecture — Intelligence Density First\n\n> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.\n\n**Goal:** Replace urllib-based ad-hoc graph queries with a unified intelligence layer: SDK-backed queries, pre-computed dimensional briefings, and agent-writable trails.\n\n**Architecture:** SurrealDB stored functions remain the canonical query layer; all callers become thin wrappers. keep all CLI commands identical\n- `scripts/graph_context_hook.py` — replace urllib internals with `graph_client.query()`\n- `metabolism/graph-briefing.md` — regenerated by GraphReactor (do not hand-edit)\n\n**Created (Phase B):**\n- `scripts/dba/0002_add_affinity_field.surql` — migration: add `dim_agent_affinity` to neuron\n- `scripts/compute_pca_matrix.py` — one-shot script: fetches all neuron titles from DB, embeds via Ollama, computes PCA, writes `scripts/dba/pca_matrix.npy`\n- `src/mcp_server/graph_write_tools.py` (in cloud-vault-mcp) — four agent-write MCP tools (latent synapse, dream synapse, affinity, annotate)\n\n**Modified (Phase B):**\n- `scripts/graph_reactor.py` — expand briefing template with affinity trails section\n- `src/mcp_server/server.py` (in cloud-vault-mcp) — register graph_write_tools\n- `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/cloud-vault-mcp/pyproject.toml` — add `numpy`, `networkx`\n- `~/.claude/mcp.json` — add surrealdb-mcp-server entry\n\n**Created (Phase C):**\n- `src/mcp_server/vault_graph/__init__.py`\n- `src/mcp_server/vault_graph/client.py` — async SurrealDB connection using SDK\n- `src/mcp_server/vault_graph/queries.py` — typed wrappers around stored functions\n- `src/mcp_server/vault_graph/reactor.py` — GraphReactor class (extracted from scripts/graph_reactor.py)\n- `src/mcp_server/vault_graph/affinity.py` — FLUME PCA pipeline\n- `src/mcp_server/vault_graph/tools.py` — 6 read MCP tools + 4 write tools (extracted from graph_write_tools.py)\n- `tests/test_vault_graph_client.py` (in cloud-vault-mcp)\n- `tests/test_vault_graph_queries.py`\n- `tests/test_vault_graph_affinity.py`\n\n**Modified (Phase C):**\n- `scripts/graph_context.py` — replace `graph_client.query()` with `vault_graph.queries.*`; keep CLI\n- `src/mcp_server/server.py` — rewire tools to vault_graph.tools\n- `src/mcp_server/graph_write_tools.py` — thin delegation to vault_graph.tools (or removed)\n\n---\n\n## Phase A — Foundation\n\n### Task 1: Install SDK and create shared graph client\n\n**Context:** Both `scripts/graph_context.py` and `scripts/graph_context_hook.py` duplicate urllib boilerplate with hardcoded `root:root` credentials. We replace them with a shared `SurrealClient` using the official Python SDK. The scripts live in the vault repo, but the SDK must also be available there via uv.\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `scripts/graph_client.py` (in vault repo)\n- Modify: `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/cloud-vault-mcp/pyproject.toml`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Add surrealdb SDK to cloud-vault-mcp**\n\n```bash\ncd /home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/cloud-vault-mcp\nuv add surrealdb networkx numpy\n```\n\nExpected: `pyproject.toml` updated with `surrealdb`, `networkx`, `numpy`; `.venv` updated.\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Write the test**\n\nCreate `tests/test_graph_client.py` in the vault repo (at `scripts/tests/`):\n\n```python\n# scripts/tests/test_graph_client.py\nimport pytest\nfrom unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock, patch\nimport sys\nimport os\n\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), \"..\"))\nfrom graph_client import SurrealClient, GraphQueryError\n\n\ndef test_surreal_client_uses_env_vars(monkeypatch):\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"SURREALDB_URL\", \"http://custom:9001\")\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"SURREALDB_USERNAME\", \"myuser\")\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"SURREALDB_PASSWORD\", \"mypass\")\n    client = SurrealClient()\n    assert client.url == \"http://custom:9001\"\n    assert client.username == \"myuser\"\n\n\ndef test_surreal_client_defaults():\n    client = SurrealClient()\n    # Falls back to root:root when env vars absent\n    assert \"8001\" in client.url\n\n\n@pytest.mark.asyncio\nasync def test_query_returns_results():\n    client = SurrealClient()\n    mock_db = AsyncMock()\n    mock_db.query.return_value = [{\"result\": [{\"id\": \"neuron:test\", \"title\": \"Test\"}]}]\n    with patch.object(client, \"_connect\", return_value=mock_db):\n        result = await client.query_async(\"SELECT * FROM neuron LIMIT 1\")\n    assert result[0][\"result\"][0][\"title\"] == \"Test\"\n\n\n@pytest.mark.asyncio\nasync def test_query_raises_on_db_error():\n    client = SurrealClient()\n    mock_db = AsyncMock()\n    mock_db.query.return_value = {\"code\": 500, \"description\": \"bad query\"}\n    with patch.object(client, \"_connect\", return_value=mock_db):\n        with pytest.raises(GraphQueryError, match=\"bad query\"):\n            await client.query_async(\"BAD SQL\")\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Run tests — expect failure**\n\n```bash\ncd /home/mike-anderson/vaults/cohezion-vault\npython -m pytest scripts/tests/test_graph_client.py -q 2>&1 | head -20\n```\n\nExpected: `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'graph_client'`\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Implement graph_client.py**\n\n```python\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nShared SurrealDB client for vault graph scripts.\n\nReads connection config from environment variables with root:root fallback\nfor local development. Production uses SURREALDB_USERNAME/PASSWORD from\nthe systemd service environment.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport asyncio\nimport os\nfrom typing import Any\n\ntry:\n    from surrealdb import Surreal\nexcept ImportError:\n    Surreal = None  # type: ignore[assignment]\n\n\nSURREAL_NS = \"cohezion\"\nSURREAL_DB = \"vault\"\n\n\nclass GraphQueryError(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised when SurrealDB returns an error response.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass SurrealClient:\n    \"\"\"Thin wrapper around surrealdb.py SDK with env-based config.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self) -> None:\n        self.url = os.environ.get(\"SURREALDB_URL\", \"http://localhost:8001\")\n        self.username = os.environ.get(\"SURREALDB_USERNAME\", \"root\")\n        self.password = os.environ.get(\"SURREALDB_PASSWORD\", \"root\")\n        self.namespace = os.environ.get(\"SURREALDB_NAMESPACE\", SURREAL_NS)\n        self.database = os.environ.get(\"SURREALDB_DATABASE\", SURREAL_DB)\n\n    async def _connect(self) -> Any:\n        db = Surreal(self.url)\n        await db.connect()\n        await db.signin({\"username\": self.username, \"password\": self.password})\n        await db.use(self.namespace, self.database)\n        return db\n\n    async def query_async(self, sql: str) -> list:\n        \"\"\"Execute SurrealQL, return result list. Raises GraphQueryError on DB errors.\"\"\"\n        db = await self._connect()\n        try:\n            data = await db.query(sql)\n        finally:\n            await db.close()\n\n        if isinstance(data, dict) and \"code\" in data:\n            raise GraphQueryError(data.get(\"description\", str(data)))\n        return data\n\n    def query(self, sql: str) -> list:\n        \"\"\"Synchronous wrapper for use in CLI scripts.\"\"\"\n        return asyncio.run(self.query_async(sql))\n\n    async def query_silent_async(self, sql: str) -> list | None:\n        \"\"\"Like query_async but returns None on any failure (for hooks).\"\"\"\n        try:\n            return await self.query_async(sql)\n        except Exception:\n            return None\n\n    def query_silent(self, sql: str) -> list | None:\n        \"\"\"Synchronous silent wrapper for hooks — never raises.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            return asyncio.run(self.query_silent_async(sql))\n        except Exception:\n            return None\n\n\n# Singleton for script use\n_client: SurrealClient | None = None\n\n\ndef get_client() -> SurrealClient:\n    global _client\n    if _client is None:\n        _client = SurrealClient()\n    return _client\n```\n\nSave to `scripts/graph_client.py`.\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Run tests — expect pass**\n\n```bash\ncd /home/mike-anderson/vaults/cohezion-vault\nuv run --project /home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/cloud-vault-mcp python -m pytest scripts/tests/test_graph_client.py -q\n```\n\nExpected: `4 passed`\n\n- [ ] **Step 6: Commit**\n\n```bash\ncd /home/mike-anderson/vaults/cohezion-vault\ngit add scripts/graph_client.py scripts/tests/test_graph_client.py\ngit commit -m \"feat(graph): shared SurrealClient using surrealdb.py SDK\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 2: Migrate graph_context.py to use SurrealClient\n\n**Context:** `graph_context.py` has one `query()` function at line 33-53 that wraps urllib. We replace it with a call to `SurrealClient.query()`. Using `option<float>` means existing neurons without values return `NONE`, not an error.\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `scripts/dba/0001_add_dim_fields.surql`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Create migration file**\n\n```surql\n-- Migration 0001: Add dimensional score fields to neuron\n-- Run: surreal import --conn http://localhost:8001 --user root --pass root --ns cohezion --db vault scripts/dba/0001_add_dim_fields.surql\n\nDEFINE FIELD dim_bridging   ON neuron TYPE option<float>\n    COMMENT \"Betweenness centrality 0-1. High = bridges clusters.\";\n\nDEFINE FIELD dim_completion ON neuron TYPE option<float>\n    COMMENT \"Note completeness heuristic 0-1. Low = stub worth fleshing out.\";\n\nDEFINE FIELD dim_recency    ON neuron TYPE option<float>\n    COMMENT \"Recency decay exp(-0.05 * days_since_modified). 1 = today, ~0.5 = 14 days ago.\";\n```\n\nSave to `scripts/dba/0001_add_dim_fields.surql`.\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Apply migration**\n\n```bash\n~/.surrealdb/surreal import \\\n  --conn http://localhost:8001 \\\n  --user root --pass root \\\n  --ns cohezion --db vault \\\n  /home/mike-anderson/vaults/cohezion-vault/scripts/dba/0001_add_dim_fields.surql\n```\n\nExpected: no errors.\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Verify fields are defined**\n\n```bash\n~/.surrealdb/surreal sql \\\n  --conn http://localhost:8001 \\\n  --user root --pass root \\\n  --ns cohezion --db vault \\\n  --hide-welcome \\\n  --query \"INFO FOR TABLE neuron;\" 2>&1 | grep dim\n```\n\nExpected: lines containing `dim_bridging`, `dim_completion`, `dim_recency`.\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Commit**\n\n```bash\ncd /home/mike-anderson/vaults/cohezion-vault\ngit add scripts/dba/0001_add_dim_fields.surql\ngit commit -m \"feat(schema): add dim_bridging, dim_completion, dim_recency to neuron\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 4: Create GraphReactor with dimension computation\n\n**Context:** `GraphReactor` runs on demand (and later on file-change events). It fetches the full synapse graph, computes betweenness centrality with networkx, then writes all three `dim_*` scores back to SurrealDB in a single batched UPDATE. Requires networkx (already added in Task 1).\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `scripts/graph_reactor.py`\n- Create: `scripts/tests/test_graph_reactor.py`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Write the tests**\n\n```python\n# scripts/tests/test_graph_reactor.py\nimport pytest\nimport math\nfrom unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch\nimport sys, os\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), \"..\"))\n\n\ndef make_mock_client(neurons, synapses):\n    client = MagicMock()\n    def query_side_effect(sql):\n        if \"FROM neuron\" in sql and \"FROM synapse\" not in sql:\n            return [{\"result\": neurons}]\n        elif \"FROM synapse\" in sql:\n            return [{\"result\": synapses}]\n        else:\n            return [{\"result\": []}]\n    client.query.side_effect = query_side_effect\n    return client\n\n\ndef test_compute_bridging_score():\n    from graph_reactor import compute_bridging_scores\n    # Linear chain: A -> B -> C — B has highest betweenness\n    edges = [(\"A\", \"B\"), (\"B\", \"C\")]\n    scores = compute_bridging_scores([\"A\", \"B\", \"C\"], edges)\n    assert scores[\"B\"] > scores[\"A\"]\n    assert scores[\"B\"] > scores[\"C\"]\n    assert all(0.0 <= v <= 1.0 for v in scores.values())\n\n\ndef test_compute_completion_score():\n    from graph_reactor import compute_completion_score\n    rich = {\"word_count\": 500, \"tags\": [\"a\", \"b\", \"c\"], \"title\": \"Full Note\", \"stage\": \"mature\"}\n    stub = {\"word_count\": 10, \"tags\": [], \"title\": \"Stub\", \"stage\": \"embryo\"}\n    assert compute_completion_score(rich) > compute_completion_score(stub)\n    assert 0.0 <= compute_completion_score(rich) <= 1.0\n\n\ndef test_compute_recency_score():\n    from graph_reactor import compute_recency_score\n    import datetime\n    today = datetime.date.today().isoformat()\n    old_date = \"2020-01-01\"\n    score_today = compute_recency_score(today)\n    score_old = compute_recency_score(old_date)\n    assert score_today > score_old\n    assert 0.0 < score_today <= 1.0\n    assert score_old >= 0.0\n\n\ndef test_recency_half_life_14_days():\n    from graph_reactor import compute_recency_score\n    import datetime\n    two_weeks_ago = (datetime.date.today() - datetime.timedelta(days=14)).isoformat()\n    score = compute_recency_score(two_weeks_ago)\n    assert 0.45 < score < 0.55  # ~0.5 at half-life\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Run tests — expect failure**\n\n```bash\ncd /home/mike-anderson/vaults/cohezion-vault\nuv run --project /home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/cloud-vault-mcp python -m pytest scripts/tests/test_graph_reactor.py -q\n```\n\nExpected: `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'graph_reactor'`\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Implement graph_reactor.py**\n\n```python\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nGraphReactor — computes dimensional scores for vault neurons and writes\nthem back to SurrealDB. Also generates metabolism/graph-briefing.md.\n\nUsage:\n    python scripts/graph_reactor.py          # compute + write dims + briefing\n    python scripts/graph_reactor.py --dims   # dims only, skip briefing\n    python scripts/graph_reactor.py --brief  # briefing only, skip dim compute\n\"\"\"\n\nimport datetime\nimport math\nimport os\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport networkx as nx\n\nfrom graph_client import get_client\n\nVAULT_ROOT = Path(__file__).parent.parent\nBRIEFING_PATH = VAULT_ROOT / \"metabolism\" / \"graph-briefing.md\"\nLAMBDA_DECAY = 0.05  # recency half-life ~14 days\n\n\n# ── Dimension computation ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef compute_bridging_scores(neuron_ids: list[str], edges: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"Betweenness centrality normalized to 0-1.\"\"\"\n    G = nx.DiGraph()\n    G.add_nodes_from(neuron_ids)\n    G.add_edges_from(edges)\n    raw = nx.betweenness_centrality(G, normalized=True)\n    # Already 0-1 when normalized=True\n    return {node: round(score, 4) for node, score in raw.items()}\n\n\ndef compute_completion_score(neuron: dict[str, Any]) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Heuristic completeness 0-1 based on word count, tags, stage.\"\"\"\n    word_count = neuron.get(\"word_count\") or 0\n    tag_count = len(neuron.get(\"tags\") or [])\n    stage = neuron.get(\"stage\", \"embryo\")\n\n    stage_score = {\"embryo\": 0.1, \"seedling\": 0.3, \"growing\": 0.6, \"mature\": 1.0}.get(stage, 0.2)\n    word_score = min(word_count / 400.0, 1.0)  # 400 words = \"complete\"\n    tag_score = min(tag_count / 4.0, 1.0)       # 4 tags = well-tagged\n\n    return round(0.5 * word_score + 0.3 * stage_score + 0.2 * tag_score, 4)\n\n\ndef compute_recency_score(modified_date: str | None) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Exponential recency decay: exp(-λ * days). λ=0.05 → half-life ~14 days.\"\"\"\n    if not modified_date:\n        return 0.0\n    try:\n        date = datetime.date.fromisoformat(modified_date[:10])\n        days = (datetime.date.today() - date).days\n        return round(math.exp(-LAMBDA_DECAY * max(days, 0)), 4)\n    except (ValueError, TypeError):\n        return 0.0\n\n\n# ── Main reactor ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef run_dimensions() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"Fetch graph, compute all dim_* scores, write to SurrealDB. Returns neuron list.\"\"\"\n    client = get_client()\n\n    neurons = client.query(\n        \"SELECT id, title, word_count, tags, stage, modified, activation, cluster_id, \"\n        \"synapse_out, synapse_in FROM neuron;\"\n    )[0].get(\"result\", [])\n\n    synapses = client.query(\n        \"SELECT VALUE [string(in), string(out)] FROM synapse WHERE type = 'explicit';\"\n    )[0].get(\"result\", [])\n\n    neuron_ids = [str(n[\"id\"]) for n in neurons]\n    edges = [(str(s[0]), str(s[1])) for s in synapses if len(s) == 2]\n\n    bridging = compute_bridging_scores(neuron_ids, edges)\n\n    for n in neurons:\n        nid = str(n[\"id\"])\n        dim_b = bridging.get(nid, 0.0)\n        dim_c = compute_completion_score(n)\n        dim_r = compute_recency_score(n.get(\"modified\"))\n        client.query(\n            f\"UPDATE {nid} SET \"\n            f\"dim_bridging = {dim_b}, \"\n            f\"dim_completion = {dim_c}, \"\n            f\"dim_recency = {dim_r};\"\n        )\n        n[\"dim_bridging\"] = dim_b\n        n[\"dim_completion\"] = dim_c\n        n[\"dim_recency\"] = dim_r\n\n    return neurons\n\n\ndef run_briefing(neurons: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Write metabolism/graph-briefing.md from neuron data.\"\"\"\n    if neurons is None:\n        client = get_client()\n        neurons = client.query(\n            \"SELECT id, title, activation, cluster_id, stage, dim_bridging, \"\n            \"dim_completion, dim_recency, synapse_out, synapse_in FROM neuron;\"\n        )[0].get(\"result\", [])\n\n    n_neurons = len(neurons)\n    client = get_client()\n    n_synapses = client.query(\"SELECT count() AS n FROM synapse GROUP ALL;\")[0].get(\"result\", [{}])[0].get(\"n\", 0)\n\n    top_bridging = sorted(\n        [n for n in neurons if n.get(\"dim_bridging\") is not None],\n        key=lambda x: x.get(\"dim_bridging\", 0),\n        reverse=True,\n    )[:5]\n\n    stubs = sorted(\n        [n for n in neurons if (n.get(\"dim_completion\") or 1) < 0.4 and (n.get(\"dim_bridging\") or 0) > 0.05],\n        key=lambda x: x.get(\"dim_bridging\", 0),\n        reverse=True,\n    )[:3]\n\n    recent = sorted(\n        [n for n in neurons if n.get(\"dim_recency\") is not None],\n        key=lambda x: x.get(\"dim_recency\", 0),\n        reverse=True,\n    )[:5]\n\n    now = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ\")\n\n    lines = [\n        f\"## Graph State — {now}\",\n        \"\",\n        f\"**Vault:** {n_neurons} neurons · {n_synapses} synapses\",\n        \"\",\n        \"**Hot neurons** (high bridging):\",\n    ]\n    for n in top_bridging:\n        lines.append(f\"- {n.get('title', '?')} (bridging={n.get('dim_bridging', 0):.3f})\")\n\n    lines += [\"\", \"**Completion gaps** (low completion, high bridging — worth fleshing out):\"]\n    for n in stubs:\n        lines.append(f\"- {n.get('title', '?')} (completion={n.get('dim_completion', 0):.2f})\")\n\n    lines += [\"\", \"**Recent activity**:\"]\n    for n in recent:\n        lines.append(f\"- {n.get('title', '?')} (recency={n.get('dim_recency', 0):.3f})\")\n\n    BRIEFING_PATH.write_text(\"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\")\n    print(f\"Briefing written to {BRIEFING_PATH}\", file=sys.stderr)\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    args = sys.argv[1:]\n    dims_only = \"--dims\" in args\n    brief_only = \"--brief\" in args\n\n    if brief_only:\n        run_briefing()\n    elif dims_only:\n        run_dimensions()\n    else:\n        neurons = run_dimensions()\n        run_briefing(neurons)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Run tests — expect pass**\n\n```bash\ncd /home/mike-anderson/vaults/cohezion-vault\nuv run --project /home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/cloud-vault-mcp python -m pytest scripts/tests/test_graph_reactor.py -q\n```\n\nExpected: `4 passed`\n\n- [ Test with a simple query via the MCP tool.\n\n---\n\n## Phase B — Compound Trails\n\n### Task 6: FLUME PCA matrix bootstrapping\n\n**Context:** We need a pre-computed PCA matrix that projects 768D `nomic-embed-text` vectors down to 12D. This is a one-shot bootstrap operation. The PCA matrix is stored at `scripts/dba/pca_matrix.npy` and checked into the vault. When neuron count grows >10%, it should be refit.\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `scripts/compute_pca_matrix.py`\n- Create: `scripts/dba/pca_matrix.npy` (generated, checked in)\n- Create: `scripts/tests/test_affinity_pca.py`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Write the tests**\n\n```python\n# scripts/tests/test_affinity_pca.py\nimport pytest\nimport numpy as np\nimport sys, os\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), \"..\"))\n\n\ndef test_pca_projection_output_shape():\n    from compute_pca_matrix import project_to_12d\n    # Random 768D vector\n    vec_768 = np.random.randn(768).tolist()\n    pca_matrix = np.random.randn(12, 768)  # dummy PCA matrix\n    result = project_to_12d(vec_768, pca_matrix)\n    assert len(result) == 12\n\n\ndef test_pca_projection_deterministic():\n    from compute_pca_matrix import project_to_12d\n    vec = [0.1] * 768\n    pca = np.ones((12, 768))\n    r1 = project_to_12d(vec, pca)\n    r2 = project_to_12d(vec, pca)\n    assert r1 == r2\n\n\ndef test_pca_projection_values_bounded():\n    \"\"\"After L2 normalization, all values are in [-1, 1].\"\"\"\n    from compute_pca_matrix import project_to_12d, normalize_l2\n    vec = np.random.randn(768).tolist()\n    pca = np.random.randn(12, 768)\n    projected = project_to_12d(vec, pca)\n    normed = normalize_l2(projected)\n    assert all(-1.0 <= v <= 1.0 for v in normed)\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Run tests — expect failure**\n\n```bash\nuv run --project /home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/cloud-vault-mcp python -m pytest scripts/tests/test_affinity_pca.py -q\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Implement compute_pca_matrix.py**\n\n```python\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nBootstrap PCA matrix for FLUME affinity pipeline.\n\nFetches all neuron titles from SurrealDB, embeds them via Ollama nomic-embed-text,\nruns PCA to get a 12D projection matrix, saves to scripts/dba/pca_matrix.npy.\n\nUsage:\n    python scripts/compute_pca_matrix.py           # full bootstrap\n    python scripts/compute_pca_matrix.py --check   # show matrix info\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport sys\nimport urllib.request\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport numpy as np\nfrom sklearn.decomposition import PCA  # type: ignore[import]\n\nfrom graph_client import get_client\n\nPCA_PATH = Path(__file__).parent / \"dba\" / \"pca_matrix.npy\"\nOLLAMA_URL = \"http://localhost:11434\"\nEMBED_MODEL = \"nomic-embed-text\"\n\n\ndef embed_text(text: str) -> list[float]:\n    \"\"\"Get 768D embedding from Ollama nomic-embed-text.\"\"\"\n    payload = json.dumps({\"model\": EMBED_MODEL, \"prompt\": text}).encode()\n    req = urllib.request.Request(\n        f\"{OLLAMA_URL}/api/embeddings\",\n        data=payload,\n        headers={\"Content-Type\": \"application/json\"},\n    )\n    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30) as resp:\n        data = json.loads(resp.read())\n    return data[\"embedding\"]\n\n\ndef project_to_12d(vec_768: list[float], pca_matrix: np.ndarray) -> list[float]:\n    \"\"\"Project 768D vector to 12D using pre-computed PCA matrix.\"\"\"\n    v = np.array(vec_768, dtype=np.float32)\n    projected = pca_matrix @ v\n    return projected.tolist()\n\n\ndef normalize_l2(vec: list[float]) -> list[float]:\n    \"\"\"L2-normalize a vector.\"\"\"\n    v = np.array(vec, dtype=np.float32)\n    norm = np.linalg.norm(v)\n    if norm < 1e-8:\n        return vec\n    return (v / norm).tolist()\n\n\ndef fit_pca(embeddings: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"Fit PCA on N×768 matrix, return 12×768 projection matrix.\"\"\"\n    pca = PCA(n_components=12, random_state=42)\n    pca.fit(embeddings)\n    return pca.components_  # shape (12, 768)\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    if \"--check\" in sys.argv:\n        if PCA_PATH.exists():\n            matrix = np.load(PCA_PATH)\n            print(f\"PCA matrix: {matrix.shape}, dtype={matrix.dtype}\")\n        else:\n            print(\"No PCA matrix found at\", PCA_PATH)\n        return\n\n    client = get_client()\n    neurons = client.query(\"SELECT id, title FROM neuron;\")[0].get(\"result\", [])\n    print(f\"Embedding {len(neurons)} neurons...\", file=sys.stderr)\n\n    embeddings = []\n    for i, n in enumerate(neurons):\n        if i % 50 == 0:\n            print(f\"  {i}/{len(neurons)}...\", file=sys.stderr)\n        emb = embed_text(n.get(\"title\", \"\"))\n        embeddings.append(emb)\n\n    matrix = np.array(embeddings, dtype=np.float32)\n    pca_matrix = fit_pca(matrix)\n    np.save(PCA_PATH, pca_matrix)\n    print(f\"PCA matrix saved: {pca_matrix.shape} → {PCA_PATH}\", file=sys.stderr)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n```\n\n> Note: `sklearn` is needed for PCA. Add it: `uv add scikit-learn` in cloud-vault-mcp.\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Add scikit-learn**\n\n```bash\ncd /home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/cloud-vault-mcp\nuv add scikit-learn\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Run tests — expect pass**\n\n```bash\nuv run --project /home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/cloud-vault-mcp python -m pytest scripts/tests/test_affinity_pca.py -q\n```\n\nExpected: `3 passed`\n\n- [ ] **Step 6: Bootstrap PCA matrix (requires Ollama + SurrealDB running)**\n\n```bash\ncd /home/mike-anderson/vaults/cohezion-vault\nuv run --project /home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/cloud-vault-mcp python scripts/compute_pca_matrix.py\n```\n\nExpected: `PCA matrix saved: (12, 768) → .../scripts/dba/pca_matrix.npy`\n\n- [ ] **Step 7: Commit**\n\n```bash\ncd /home/mike-anderson/vaults/cohezion-vault\ngit add scripts/compute_pca_matrix.py scripts/tests/test_affinity_pca.py scripts/dba/pca_matrix.npy\ngit commit -m \"feat(flume): bootstrap PCA matrix for dim_agent_affinity (12D)\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 7: Schema migration for dim_agent_affinity\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `scripts/dba/0002_add_affinity_field.surql`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Create migration file**\n\n```surql\n-- Migration 0002: Add agent affinity vector field to neuron\n-- Run: surreal import --conn http://localhost:8001 --user root --pass root --ns cohezion --db vault scripts/dba/0002_add_affinity_field.surql\n\nDEFINE FIELD dim_agent_affinity ON neuron TYPE option<array<float>>\n    COMMENT \"12D FLUME affinity vector from agent sessions. Aggregated over time.\";\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Apply migration**\n\n```bash\n~/.surrealdb/surreal import \\\n  --conn http://localhost:8001 \\\n  --user root --pass root \\\n  --ns cohezion --db vault \\\n  /home/mike-anderson/vaults/cohezion-vault/scripts/dba/0002_add_affinity_field.surql\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Commit**\n\n```bash\ncd /home/mike-anderson/vaults/cohezion-vault\ngit add scripts/dba/0002_add_affinity_field.surql\ngit commit -m \"feat(schema): add dim_agent_affinity array field to neuron\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 8: Agent-write MCP tools (cloud-vault-mcp)\n\n**Context:** Four new tools let agents write to the graph without touching structural fields. These live in `cloud-vault-mcp` and use the existing `SurrealDBSync` connection. For now they call directly via the existing surrealdb_sync module; they'll be moved to `vault_graph/` in Phase C.\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `src/mcp_server/graph_write_tools.py` (in cloud-vault-mcp)\n- Create: `tests/test_graph_write_tools.py` (in cloud-vault-mcp)\n- Modify: `src/mcp_server/server.py`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Write the tests**\n\nCreate `tests/test_graph_write_tools.py` in cloud-vault-mcp:\n\n```python\n# tests/test_graph_write_tools.py\nimport pytest\nfrom unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock, patch\nimport sys, os\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), \"../src\"))\n\nfrom mcp_server.graph_write_tools import (\n    _validate_not_explicit_synapse,\n    build_latent_synapse_sql,\n    build_affinity_update_sql,\n    build_annotate_sql,\n    GraphWriteError,\n)\n\n\ndef test_latent_synapse_sql_format():\n    sql = build_latent_synapse_sql(\"neuron:abc\", \"neuron:xyz\", \"test reason\")\n    assert \"RELATE\" in sql\n    assert \"neuron:abc\" in sql\n    assert \"neuron:xyz\" in sql\n    assert \"'latent'\" in sql\n\n\ndef test_affinity_update_sql():\n    vec = [0.1] * 12\n    sql = build_affinity_update_sql(\"neuron:abc\", vec)\n    assert \"UPDATE neuron:abc\" in sql\n    assert \"dim_agent_affinity\" in sql\n\n\ndef test_annotate_sql_only_safe_fields():\n    sql = build_annotate_sql(\"neuron:abc\", last_accessed=\"2026-01-01\", agent_notes=\"test\")\n    assert \"last_accessed\" in sql\n    assert \"agent_notes\" in sql\n    # Must not touch structural fields\n    assert \"title\" not in sql\n    assert \"path\" not in sql\n\n\ndef test_validate_not_explicit_raises():\n    mock_client = MagicMock()\n    mock_client.query.return_value = [{\"result\": [{\"type\": \"explicit\"}]}]\n    with pytest.raises(GraphWriteError, match=\"explicit synapse\"):\n        _validate_not_explicit_synapse(mock_client, \"neuron:a\", \"neuron:b\")\n\n\ndef test_validate_not_explicit_passes_when_empty():\n    mock_client = MagicMock()\n    mock_client.query.return_value = [{\"result\": []}]\n    # Should not raise\n    _validate_not_explicit_synapse(mock_client, \"neuron:a\", \"neuron:b\")\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Run tests — expect failure**\n\n```bash\ncd /home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/cloud-vault-mcp\nuv run pytest tests/test_graph_write_tools.py -q\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Implement graph_write_tools.py**\n\n```python\n\"\"\"\nAgent-write MCP tools for the vault graph.\n\nAgents may write: latent synapses, dream synapses, affinity vectors,\nand metadata annotations. They may NOT overwrite explicit (structural) synapses\nor structural neuron fields (title, path, tags, stage).\n\"\"\"\n\nimport logging\nfrom typing import Any\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nclass GraphWriteError(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised when a graph write would violate write boundaries.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef _validate_not_explicit_synapse(client: Any, from_id: str, to_id: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Raise if an explicit synapse already exists between these neurons.\"\"\"\n    sql = (\n        f\"SELECT type FROM synapse \"\n        f\"WHERE in = {from_id} AND out = {to_id} AND type = 'explicit';\"\n    )\n    results = client.query(sql)\n    hits = results[0].get(\"result\", [])\n    if hits:\n        raise GraphWriteError(\n            f\"An explicit synapse already exists between {from_id} and {to_id}. \"\n            \"Agents may not overwrite structural synapses.\"\n        )\n\n\ndef build_latent_synapse_sql(from_id: str, to_id: str, reason: str) -> str:\n    reason_esc = reason.replace(\"'\", \"\\\\'\")\n    return (\n        f\"RELATE {from_id}->{to_id} SET type = 'latent', reason = '{reason_esc}', \"\n        f\"created_at = time::now();\"\n    )\n\n\ndef build_dream_synapse_sql(from_id: str, to_id: str, resonance: str) -> str:\n    resonance_esc = resonance.replace(\"'\", \"\\\\'\")\n    return (\n        f\"RELATE {from_id}->{to_id} SET type = 'dream', resonance = '{resonance_esc}', \"\n        f\"created_at = time::now();\"\n    )\n\n\ndef build_affinity_update_sql(neuron_id: str, vec_12d: list[float]) -> str:\n    vec_str = \"[\" + \", \".join(str(v) for v in vec_12d) + \"]\"\n    return f\"UPDATE {neuron_id} SET dim_agent_affinity = {vec_str};\"\n\n\ndef build_annotate_sql(\n    neuron_id: str,\n    last_accessed: str | None = None,\n    agent_notes: str | None = None,\n    access_count_delta: int = 0,\n) -> str:\n    sets = []\n    if last_accessed:\n        sets.append(f\"last_accessed = '{last_accessed}'\")\n    if agent_notes:\n        notes_esc = agent_notes.replace(\"'\", \"\\\\'\")\n        sets.append(f\"agent_notes = '{notes_esc}'\")\n    if access_count_delta:\n        sets.append(f\"access_count += {access_count_delta}\")\n    if not sets:\n        return \"\"\n    return f\"UPDATE {neuron_id} SET {', '.join(sets)};\"\n\n\ndef register_graph_write_tools(mcp: Any, get_surrealdb_client: Any) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Register the four agent-write tools with the FastMCP instance.\"\"\"\n\n    @mcp.tool()\n    def graph_write_latent_synapse(from_neuron_id: str, to_neuron_id: str, reason: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Create a latent (semantically inferred) synapse between two neurons.\n\n        Args:\n            from_neuron_id: Source neuron ID (e.g. 'neuron:cortex_foo_md')\n            to_neuron_id: Target neuron ID\n            reason: Why this connection exists (stored on the synapse)\n        \"\"\"\n        client = get_surrealdb_client()\n        _validate_not_explicit_synapse(client, from_neuron_id, to_neuron_id)\n        sql = build_latent_synapse_sql(from_neuron_id, to_neuron_id, reason)\n        client.query(sql)\n        return f\"Latent synapse created: {from_neuron_id} -> {to_neuron_id}\"\n\n    @mcp.tool()\n    def graph_write_dream_synapse(from_neuron_id: str, to_neuron_id: str, resonance: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Create a dream synapse (cross-domain resonance) between two neurons.\n\n        Args:\n            from_neuron_id: Source neuron ID\n            to_neuron_id: Target neuron ID\n            resonance: Description of the cross-domain connection\n        \"\"\"\n        client = get_surrealdb_client()\n        _validate_not_explicit_synapse(client, from_neuron_id, to_neuron_id)\n        sql = build_dream_synapse_sql(from_neuron_id, to_neuron_id, resonance)\n        client.query(sql)\n        return f\"Dream synapse created: {from_neuron_id} -> {to_neuron_id}\"\n\n    @mcp.tool()\n    def graph_write_affinity(neuron_id: str, affinity_vector: list[float]) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Write a 12D FLUME affinity vector to a neuron.\n\n        Args:\n            neuron_id: Target neuron ID\n            affinity_vector: 12-element float list (L2-normalized)\n        \"\"\"\n        if len(affinity_vector) != 12:\n            return f\"Error: affinity_vector must have exactly 12 elements, got {len(affinity_vector)}\"\n        client = get_surrealdb_client()\n        sql = build_affinity_update_sql(neuron_id, affinity_vector)\n        client.query(sql)\n        return f\"Affinity vector written to {neuron_id}\"\n\n    @mcp.tool()\n    def graph_annotate_neuron(\n        neuron_id: str,\n        last_accessed: str = \"\",\n        agent_notes: str = \"\",\n        increment_access_count: bool = False,\n    ) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Annotate a neuron with agent metadata (does not touch structural fields).\n\n        Args:\n            neuron_id: Target neuron ID\n            last_accessed: ISO date string (e.g. '2026-03-20')\n            agent_notes: Free-form notes from the agent session\n            increment_access_count: Whether to increment access_count by 1\n        \"\"\"\n        client = get_surrealdb_client()\n        sql = build_annotate_sql(\n            neuron_id,\n            last_accessed=last_accessed or None,\n            agent_notes=agent_notes or None,\n            access_count_delta=1 if increment_access_count else 0,\n        )\n        if not sql:\n            return \"No fields to update.\"\n        client.query(sql)\n        return f\"Annotations written to {neuron_id}\"\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Register in server.py**\n\nIn `src/mcp_server/server.py`, after the `agent_context` block (around line 49), add:\n\n```python\n    from .graph_write_tools import register_graph_write_tools\n    if config.surrealdb_enabled and surrealdb:\n        register_graph_write_tools(mcp, lambda: surrealdb)\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Run tests — expect pass**\n\n```bash\ncd /home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/cloud-vault-mcp\nuv run pytest tests/test_graph_write_tools.py -q\n```\n\nExpected: `5 vault_graph/client.py**\n\n```python\n\"\"\"Async SurrealDB client for vault_graph module.\"\"\"\n\nimport os\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom surrealdb import Surreal\n\n\nclass GraphQueryError(Exception):\n    \"\"\"SurrealDB returned an error response.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass GraphClient:\n    def __init__(self) -> None:\n        self.url = os.environ.get(\"SURREALDB_URL\", \"http://localhost:8001\")\n        self.username = os.environ.get(\"SURREALDB_USERNAME\", \"root\")\n        self.password = os.environ.get(\"SURREALDB_PASSWORD\", \"root\")\n        self.namespace = os.environ.get(\"SURREALDB_NAMESPACE\", \"cohezion\")\n        self.database = os.environ.get(\"SURREALDB_DATABASE\", \"vault\")\n\n    async def _make_connection(self) -> Any:\n        db = Surreal(self.url)\n        await db.connect()\n        await db.signin({\"username\": self.username, \"password\": self.password})\n        await db.use(self.namespace, self.database)\n        return db\n\n    async def query(self, sql: str) -> list:\n        \"\"\"Execute SurrealQL, unwrap first result set, raise on error.\"\"\"\n        db = await self._make_connection()\n        try:\n            data = await db.query(sql)\n        finally:\n            await db.close()\n        if isinstance(data, dict) and \"code\" in data:\n            raise GraphQueryError(data.get(\"description\", str(data)))\n        # Unwrap: [{\"result\": [...]}] → [...]\n        if data and isinstance(data, list) and \"result\" in data[0]:\n            return data[0][\"result\"]\n        return data or []\n\n    async def execute(self, sql: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Execute SurrealQL without returning results (for writes).\"\"\"\n        await self.query(sql)\n\n\n_client: GraphClient | None = None\n\n\ndef get_graph_client() -> GraphClient:\n    global _client\n    if _client is None:\n        _client = GraphClient()\n    return _client\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Implement vault_graph/queries.py**\n\n```python\n\"\"\"Thin async wrappers around SurrealDB stored functions.\"\"\"\n\nfrom .client import GraphClient, get_graph_client\n\n\nasync def neighborhood(neuron_id: str, client: GraphClient | None = None) -> dict:\n    c = client or get_graph_client()\n    results = await c.query(f\"SELECT * FROM fn::context_neighborhood({neuron_id});\")\n    return results[0] if results else {}\n\n\nasync def search(query: str, client: GraphClient | None = None) -> list:\n    c = client or get_graph_client()\n    q = query.replace(\"'\", \"\\\\'\")\n    return await c.query(f\"SELECT * FROM fn::context_search('{q}');\")\n\n\nasync def cluster(cluster_name: str, client: GraphClient | None = None) -> dict:\n    c = client or get_graph_client()\n    name = cluster_name.replace(\"'\", \"\\\\'\")\n    results = await c.query(f\"SELECT * FROM fn::context_cluster('{name}');\")\n    return results[0] if results else {}\n\n\nasync def hops(neuron_id: str, depth: int = 2, client: GraphClient | None = None) -> list:\n    c = client or get_graph_client()\n    return await c.query(f\"SELECT * FROM fn::context_hops({neuron_id}, {depth});\")\n\n\nasync def bridges(cluster_a: str, cluster_b: str, client: GraphClient | None = None) -> list:\n    c = client or get_graph_client()\n    a = cluster_a.replace(\"'\", \"\\\\'\")\n    b = cluster_b.replace(\"'\", \"\\\\'\")\n    return await c.query(f\"SELECT * FROM fn::context_bridges('{a}', '{b}');\")\n\n\nasync def stats(client: GraphClient | None = None) -> dict:\n    c = client or get_graph_client()\n    results = await c.query(\"SELECT * FROM fn::vault_stats();\")\n    return results[0] if results else {}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 6: Create vault_graph/__init__.py**\n\n```python\n\"\"\"vault_graph — typed wrappers around the SurrealDB graph intelligence layer.\"\"\"\n\nfrom .client import GraphClient, GraphQueryError, get_graph_client\nfrom . import queries\n\n__all__ = [\"GraphClient\", \"GraphQueryError\", \"get_graph_client\", \"queries\"]\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 7: Run tests — expect pass**\n\n```bash\ncd /home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/cloud-vault-mcp\nuv run pytest functions.\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nfrom . import queries\n\n\nasync def tool_graph_neighborhood(neuron_id: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Return the local neighborhood of a neuron: metadata, links, cluster siblings.\n\n    Args:\n        neuron_id: Full neuron ID (e.g. 'neuron:cortex_foo_md')\n    \"\"\"\n    data = await queries.neighborhood(neuron_id)\n    if not data:\n        return f\"No neuron found: {neuron_id}\"\n    n = data.get(\"neuron\", {})\n    lines = [\n        f\"=== {n.get('title', '?')} ===\",\n        f\"Activation: {n.get('activation', 0):.3f} | Stage: {n.get('stage', '?')} | Cluster: {n.get('cluster_id', '?')}\",\n    ]\n    for link in data.get(\"outbound\", [])[:5]:\n        lines.append(f\"  -> {link.get('activation', 0):.2f} {link.get('title', '?')}\")\n    for link in data.get(\"inbound\", [])[:5]:\n        lines.append(f\"  <- {link.get('activation', 0):.2f} {link.get('title', '?')}\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\nasync def tool_graph_search(query: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Search neurons by title/tag substring match.\n\n    Args:\n        query: Search term (e.g. 'quantum', 'attention mechanism')\n    \"\"\"\n    hits = await queries.search(query)\n    if not hits:\n        return f\"No neurons matching '{query}'\"\n    lines = [f\"=== Search: '{query}' ({len(hits)} results) ===\"]\n    for n in hits[:15]:\n        lines.append(f\"  {n.get('activation', 0):.2f} {n.get('title', '?')} ({n.get('cluster_id', '?')})\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\nasync def tool_graph_cluster(cluster_name: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Return cluster summary: top neurons, stats, coherence.\n\n    Args:\n        cluster_name: Cluster ID (e.g. 'cortex', 'sensory', 'prefrontal')\n    \"\"\"\n    data = await queries.cluster(cluster_name)\n    if not data:\n        return f\"Cluster not found: {cluster_name}\"\n    lines = [\n        f\"=== Cluster: {data.get('cluster', cluster_name)} ===\",\n        f\"Neurons: {data.get('total_neurons', 0)} | Avg Activation: {data.get('avg_activation', 0):.3f}\",\n    ]\n    for n in data.get(\"top_neurons\", [])[:10]:\n        lines.append(f\"  {n.get('activation', 0):.2f} {n.get('title', '?')}\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\nasync def tool_graph_hops(neuron_id: str, depth: int = 2) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Return all neurons reachable within N hops.\n\n    Args:\n        neuron_id: Starting neuron ID\n        depth: Number of hops (1 or 2, default 2)\n    \"\"\"\n    hits = await queries.hops(neuron_id, depth)\n    if not hits:\n        return f\"No neurons within {depth} hops of {neuron_id}\"\n    lines = [f\"=== {depth}-hop neighborhood ({len(hits)} neurons) ===\"]\n    for n in hits[:20]:\n        lines.append(f\"  {n.get('activation', 0):.2f} {n.get('title', '?')} ({n.get('cluster_id', '?')})\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\nasync def tool_graph_bridges(cluster_a: str, cluster_b: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Find neurons bridging two clusters.\n\n    Args:\n        cluster_a: First cluster ID\n        cluster_b: Second cluster ID\n    \"\"\"\n    hits = await queries.bridges(cluster_a, cluster_b)\n    if not hits:\n        return f\"No bridges between {cluster_a} and {cluster_b}\"\n    lines = [f\"=== Bridges: {cluster_a} <-> {cluster_b} ({len(hits)}) ===\"]\n    for n in hits[:10]:\n        lines.append(f\"  {n.get('activation', 0):.2f} {n.get('title', '?')}\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\nasync def tool_graph_stats() -> str:\n    \"\"\"Return global vault statistics snapshot.\"\"\"\n    data = await queries.stats()\n    if not data:\n        return \"Could not fetch vault stats\"\n    lines = [\n        \"=== Vault Stats ===\",\n        f\"Neurons: {data.get('total_neurons', '?')} | Synapses: {data.get('total_synapses', '?')}\",\n    ]\n    for s in data.get(\"stage_distribution\", []):\n        lines.append(f\"  {s.get('stage', '?')}: {s.get('n', 0)}\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\ndef register_read_tools(mcp):\n    \"\"\"Register all 6 read tools with the FastMCP instance.\"\"\"\n\n    @mcp.tool()\n    async def graph_neighborhood(neuron_id: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Return the local neighborhood of a neuron (metadata, links, cluster siblings).\"\"\"\n        return await tool_graph_neighborhood(neuron_id)\n\n    @mcp.tool()\n    async def graph_search(query: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Search neurons by title/tag substring match.\"\"\"\n        return await tool_graph_search(query)\n\n    @mcp.tool()\n    async def graph_cluster(cluster_name: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Return cluster summary: top neurons, count, average activation.\"\"\"\n        return await tool_graph_cluster(cluster_name)\n\n    @mcp.tool()\n    async def graph_hops(neuron_id: str, depth: int = 2) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Return all neurons reachable within N hops of a neuron.\"\"\"\n        return await tool_graph_hops(neuron_id, depth)\n\n    @mcp.tool()\n    async def graph_bridges(cluster_a: str, cluster_b: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Find neurons that bridge two clusters (cross-domain connectors).\"\"\"\n        return await tool_graph_bridges(cluster_a, cluster_b)\n\n    @mcp.tool()\n    async def graph_stats() -> str:\n        \"\"\"Return global vault statistics: neuron/synapse counts, stage distribution.\"\"\"\n        return await tool_graph_stats()\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Register read tools in server.py**\n\nIn `src/mcp_server/server.py`, alongside the write tools registration:\n\n```python\n    from .vault_graph.tools import register_read_tools\n    register_read_tools(mcp)\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Run tests — expect pass**\n\n```bash\nuv run pytest tests/test_vault_graph_tools.py tests/test_vault_graph_client.py tests/test_vault_graph_queries.py -q\n```\n\nExpected: all pass.\n\n- [ ] **Step 6: Run full test suite**\n\n```bash\nuv run pytest -q 2>&1 | tail -5\n```\n\nExpected: pass rate same as before + new tests passing. No regressions.\n\n- [ ] **Step 7: Restart service and smoke-test**\n\n```bash\nsystemctl --user restart cohezion-vault.service && sleep 3\ncurl -s -H \"Authorization: Bearer REDACTED\" \\\n  http://localhost:8360/tools | python3 -c \"\nimport sys, json\ntools = [t['name'] for t in json.load(sys.stdin)]\ngraph_tools = [t for t in tools if t.startswith('graph_')]\nprint(sorted(graph_tools))\n\"\n```\n\nExpected: `['graph_annotate_neuron', 'graph_bridges', 'graph_cluster', 'graph_hops', 'graph_neighborhood', 'graph_search', 'graph_stats', 'graph_write_affinity', 'graph_write_dream_synapse', 'graph_write_latent_synapse']`\n\n- [ ] **Step 8: Commit**\n\n```bash\ncd /home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/cloud-vault-mcp\ngit add src/mcp_server/vault_graph/tools.py src/mcp_server/server.py tests/test_vault_graph_tools.py\ngit commit -m \"feat(vault_graph): 6 read MCP tools — neighborhood/search/cluster/hops/bridges/stats\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Verification Checklist\n\nAfter all tasks complete, verify against spec success criteria:\n\n```bash\n# 1.      1→# Graph Architecture — Intelligence Density First
     2→
     3→> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.
     4→
     5→**Goal:** Replace urllib-based ad-hoc graph queries with a unified intelligence layer: SDK-backed queries, pre-computed dimensional briefings, and agent-writable trails.
     6→
     7→**Architecture:** SurrealDB stored functions remain the canonical query layer; all callers become thin wrappers. A new `GraphReactor` pre-computes dimensional scores (`dim_bridging`, `dim_completion`, `dim_recency`) on file-change events and writes them to `metabolism/graph-briefing.md` so agent sessions pay zero query cost at start. Phase B adds FLUME affinity trails; Phase C extracts `vault_graph/` as a proper importable module.

{
