  "old_string": "### Task 10: Six read MCP tools + rewiring",
  "new_string": "### Task 10: Extract reactor.py and affinity.py into vault_graph/\n\n**Context:** Move the dimension-computation logic from `scripts/graph_reactor.py` and `scripts/compute_pca_matrix.py` into importable modules inside `vault_graph/`. The scripts keep their CLI entry points but delegate to the module. This completes the `vault_graph/` module with all five planned files.\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `src/mcp_server/vault_graph/reactor.py`\n- Create: `src/mcp_server/vault_graph/affinity.py`\n- Create: `tests/test_vault_graph_affinity.py`\n- Modify: `scripts/graph_reactor.py` — delegate to `vault_graph.reactor`\n- Modify: `scripts/compute_pca_matrix.py` — delegate to `vault_graph.affinity`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Write affinity tests**\n\n```python\n# tests/test_vault_graph_affinity.py\nimport pytest\nimport numpy as np\nimport sys, os\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), \"../src\"))\n\nfrom mcp_server.vault_graph.affinity import project_to_12d, normalize_l2\n\n\ndef test_project_to_12d_shape():\n    vec = [0.1] * 768\n    matrix = np.random.randn(12, 768).astype(np.float32)\n    result = project_to_12d(vec, matrix)\n    assert len(result) == 12\n\n\ndef test_project_to_12d_deterministic():\n    vec = [0.5] * 768\n    matrix = np.eye(12, 768, dtype=np.float32)\n    assert project_to_12d(vec, matrix) == project_to_12d(vec, matrix)\n\n\ndef test_normalize_l2_unit_length():\n    vec = [3.0, 4.0, 0.0]\n    normed = normalize_l2(vec)\n    length = sum(v**2 for v in normed) ** 0.5\n    assert abs(length - 1.0) < 1e-6\n\n\ndef test_normalize_l2_zero_vector_safe():\n    vec = [0.0, 0.0, 0.0]\n    result = normalize_l2(vec)\n    assert result == [0.0, 0.0, 0.0]\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Run tests — expect failure**\n\n```bash\ncd /home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/cloud-vault-mcp\nuv run pytest tests/test_vault_graph_affinity.py -q\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Implement vault_graph/affinity.py**\n\n```python\n\"\"\"FLUME PCA affinity pipeline — importable module extracted from compute_pca_matrix.py.\"\"\"\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport numpy as np\n\nPCA_PATH = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent.parent.parent / \"vaults\" / \"cohezion-vault\" / \"scripts\" / \"dba\" / \"pca_matrix.npy\"\n# Override via env var for portability:\nimport os\n_pca_env = os.environ.get(\"VAULT_PCA_MATRIX_PATH\")\nif _pca_env:\n    PCA_PATH = Path(_pca_env)\n\n\ndef load_pca_matrix() -> np.ndarray | None:\n    \"\"\"Load the pre-computed PCA matrix. Returns None if not found.\"\"\"\n    if PCA_PATH.exists():\n        return np.load(str(PCA_PATH)).astype(np.float32)\n    return None\n\n\ndef project_to_12d(vec_768: list[float], pca_matrix: np.ndarray) -> list[float]:\n    \"\"\"Project a 768D embedding vector to 12D using the PCA matrix.\"\"\"\n    v = np.array(vec_768, dtype=np.float32)\n    return (pca_matrix @ v).tolist()\n\n\ndef normalize_l2(vec: list[float]) -> list[float]:\n    \"\"\"L2-normalize a vector. Returns zeros if input is zero vector.\"\"\"\n    v = np.array(vec, dtype=np.float32)\n    norm = float(np.linalg.norm(v))\n    if norm < 1e-8:\n        return [0.0] * len(vec)\n    return (v / norm).tolist()\n\n\ndef embed_and_project(text: str, pca_matrix: np.ndarray, ollama_url: str = \"http://localhost:11434\") -> list[float] | None:\n    \"\"\"Embed text via Ollama nomic-embed-text, project to 12D. Returns None on failure.\"\"\"\n    import json\n    import urllib.request\n    try:\n        payload = json.dumps({\"model\": \"nomic-embed-text\", \"prompt\": text}).encode()\n        req = urllib.request.Request(\n            f\"{ollama_url}/api/embeddings\",\n            data=payload,\n            headers={\"Content-Type\": \"application/json\"},\n        )\n        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=10) as resp:\n            emb = json.loads(resp.read())[\"embedding\"]\n        projected = project_to_12d(emb, pca_matrix)\n        return normalize_l2(projected)\n    except Exception:\n        return None\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Implement vault_graph/reactor.py**\n\n```python\n\"\"\"GraphReactor — importable module extracted from scripts/graph_reactor.py.\n\nContains the pure computation logic. The scripts/graph_reactor.py CLI wrapper\ndelegates to this module.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport datetime\nimport math\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport networkx as nx\n\nLAMBDA_DECAY = 0.05\n\n\ndef compute_bridging_scores(neuron_ids: list[str], edges: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"Betweenness centrality normalized 0-1.\"\"\"\n    G = nx.DiGraph()\n    G.add_nodes_from(neuron_ids)\n    G.add_edges_from(edges)\n    raw = nx.betweenness_centrality(G, normalized=True)\n    return {node: round(score, 4) for node, score in raw.items()}\n\n\ndef compute_completion_score(neuron: dict[str, Any]) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Heuristic completeness 0-1.\"\"\"\n    word_count = neuron.get(\"word_count\") or 0\n    tag_count = len(neuron.get(\"tags\") or [])\n    stage = neuron.get(\"stage\", \"embryo\")\n    stage_score = {\"embryo\": 0.1, \"seedling\": 0.3, \"growing\": 0.6, \"mature\": 1.0}.get(stage, 0.2)\n    word_score = min(word_count / 400.0, 1.0)\n    tag_score = min(tag_count / 4.0, 1.0)\n    return round(0.5 * word_score + 0.3 * stage_score + 0.2 * tag_score, 4)\n\n\ndef compute_recency_score(modified_date: str | None) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Exponential recency decay exp(-0.05 * days). Half-life ~14 days.\"\"\"\n    if not modified_date:\n        return 0.0\n    try:\n        date = datetime.date.fromisoformat(modified_date[:10])\n        days = (datetime.date.today() - date).days\n        return round(math.exp(-LAMBDA_DECAY * max(days, 0)), 4)\n    except (ValueError, TypeError):\n        return 0.0\n\n\ndef build_briefing(neurons: list[dict[str, Any]], n_synapses: int) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Build the metabolism/graph-briefing.md content string.\"\"\"\n    now = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ\")\n    n_neurons = len(neurons)\n\n    top_bridging = sorted(\n        [n for n in neurons if n.get(\"dim_bridging\") is not None],\n        key=lambda x: x.get(\"dim_bridging\", 0), reverse=True,\n    )[:5]\n\n    stubs = sorted(\n        [n for n in neurons if (n.get(\"dim_completion\") or 1) < 0.4 and (n.get(\"dim_bridging\") or 0) > 0.05],\n        key=lambda x: x.get(\"dim_bridging\", 0), reverse=True,\n    )[:3]\n\n    recent =        lines.append(f\"- {n.get('title', '?')} (recency={n.get('dim_recency', 0):.3f})\")\n\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\"\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Run affinity tests — expect
